[ADR 0001](../../decisions/product.md#adr-0001) keeps failures with the owning typed operation. [ADR 0018](../../decisions/core-data-io.md#adr-0018) supplies [`ConfigFile.Load`](../../classes/ConfigFile.md#m-electron2d-configfile-load-system-string) for configuration files; the product exposes no database read API corresponding to this generic reference code. |
| [`enum_value ERR_DATABASE_CANT_WRITE [Error] = 35`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | [Pinned value](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml#L2763). [ADR 0001](../../decisions/product.md#adr-0001) keeps failures with the owning typed operation. [ADR 0018](../../decisions/core-data-io.md#adr-0018) supplies [`ConfigFile.Save`](../../classes/ConfigFile.md#m-electron2d-configfile-save-system-string) for configuration files; the product exposes no database write API corresponding to this generic reference code. |
| [`enum_value ERR_DOES_NOT_EXIST [Error] = 33`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | [Pinned value](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml#L2757). [ADR 0093](../../decisions/scene-animation.md#adr-0093) exposes a missing entry as `KeyNotFoundException` from [`AnimationLibrary.GetAnimation`](../../classes/AnimationLibrary.md) and a separate boolean membership query through `HasAnimation`. |
| [`enum_value ERR_DUPLICATE_SYMBOL [Error] = 42`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | [Pinned value](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml#L2784). [ADR 0091](../../decisions/scripting.md#adr-0091) leaves duplicate C# symbols to compile diagnostics. The runtime [`Script`](../../classes/Script.md) does not compile user source at runtime. |
| [`enum_value ERR_FILE_ALREADY_IN_USE [Error] = 11`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | [Pinned value](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml#L2691). [ADR 0020](../../decisions/core-data-io.md#adr-0020) preserves sharing violations from [`FileAccess.Open`](../../classes/FileAccess.md) as `IOException` with the operating-system reason where the filesystem enforces exclusive access. |
| [`enum_value ERR_FILE_BAD_DRIVE [Error] = 8`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | [Pinned value](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml#L2682). [ADR 0022](../../decisions/core-data-io.md#adr-0022) passes drive/path failures from [`DirAccess.Open`](../../classes/DirAccess.md) through filesystem exceptions. Drive enumeration remains a domain query through [`DirAccess.GetDriveCount`](../../classes/DirAccess.md). |
| [`enum_value ERR_FILE_BAD_PATH [Error] = 9`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | [Pinned value](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml#L2685). [ADR 0022](../../decisions/core-data-io.md#adr-0022) reports an unavailable directory through the filesystem exception from [`DirAccess.Open`](../../classes/DirAccess.md); its argument and virtual-root validation remains separate. |
| [`enum_value ERR_FILE_CANT_OPEN [Error] = 12`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | [Pinned value](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml#L2694). [ADR 0018](../../decisions/core-data-io.md#adr-0018) adapts [the declared ConfigFile open error](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/ConfigFile.xml#L153-L160) to the original filesystem exception from [`ConfigFile.Load`](../../classes/ConfigFile.md#m-electron2d-configfile-load-system-string), distinguishing missing, inaccessible and otherwise unreadable input. |
| [`enum_value ERR_FILE_CANT_READ [Error] = 14`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | [Pinned value](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml#L2700). [ADR 0020](../../decisions/core-data-io.md#adr-0020) preserves file read failures from [`FileAccess.ReadBytes`](../../classes/FileAccess.md). Byte counts and EOF remain stream results, independent of a native read failure. |
| [`enum_value ERR_FILE_CANT_WRITE [Error] = 13`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | [Pinned value](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml#L2697). [ADR 0018](../../decisions/core-data-io.md#adr-0018) preserves the write/replace failure from [`ConfigFile.Save`](../../classes/ConfigFile.md#m-electron2d-configfile-save-system-string); atomic replacement keeps the preceding destination on pre-replacement failure. |
| [`enum_value ERR_FILE_CORRUPT [Error] = 16`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | [Pinned value](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml#L2706). [ADR 0013](../../decisions/resources.md#adr-0013) rejects malformed archive payloads from [`ResourceLoader.Load`](../../classes/ResourceLoader.md#load) with format-specific exceptions such as `InvalidDataException`. This differs from a missing or unsupported file. |
| [`enum_value ERR_FILE_EOF [Error] = 18`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | [Pinned value](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml#L2712). [ADR 0049](../../decisions/core-data-io.md#adr-0049) returns false from [`XMLParser.Read`](../../classes/XMLParser.md#read) at input exhaustion; [ADR 0020](../../decisions/core-data-io.md#adr-0020) uses [`FileAccess.EOFReached`](../../classes/FileAccess.md) and a separate exact-read exception for truncated binary values. |
| [`enum_value ERR_FILE_MISSING_DEPENDENCIES [Error] = 17`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | [Pinned value](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml#L2709). [ADR 0013](../../decisions/resources.md#adr-0013) retains missing dependency policy in [`ResourceLoader.SetAbortOnMissingResources`](../../classes/ResourceLoader.md#setabortonmissingresources) and propagates the failed dependency through [`ResourceLoader.Load`](../../classes/ResourceLoader.md#load) when abort is enabled. |
| [`enum_value ERR_FILE_NOT_FOUND [Error] = 7`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | [Pinned value](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml#L2679). [ADR 0018](../../decisions/core-data-io.md#adr-0018) preserves `FileNotFoundException` from [`ConfigFile.Load`](../../classes/ConfigFile.md#m-electron2d-configfile-load-system-string) for a missing file in an existing directory. |
| [`enum_value ERR_FILE_NO_PERMISSION [Error] = 10`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | [Pinned value](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml#L2688). [ADR 0020](../../decisions/core-data-io.md#adr-0020) preserves filesystem access errors from [`FileAccess.Open`](../../classes/FileAccess.md); callers can catch `UnauthorizedAccessException` when the operating system denies permission. |
| [`enum_value ERR_FILE_UNRECOGNIZED [Error] = 15`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | [Pinned value](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml#L2703). Under [ADR 0039](../../decisions/resources.md#adr-0039), [the reference image decoder hook](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/ImageFormatLoaderExtension.xml) projects unrecognized contents to `false` from [`ImageFormatLoaderExtension.LoadImage`](../../classes/ImageFormatLoaderExtension.md#construction-and-extension-hooks). [`Image.Load`](../../classes/Image.md#load) discards temporary pixels and tries the next decoder at offset zero. A typed exception propagates and ends the load immediately. Exhausting the matching decoders yields `NotSupportedException`. Under [ADR 0013](../../decisions/resources.md#adr-0013), [`ResourceLoader.Load`](../../classes/ResourceLoader.md#load) also throws `NotSupportedException` for an unsupported requested resource type with no matching loader. |
| [`enum_value ERR_HELP [Error] = 46`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | [Pinned value](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml#L2796). [ADR 0004](../../decisions/product.md#adr-0004) and [ADR 0016](../../decisions/core-object-runtime.md#adr-0016) expose a hosted [`Engine.Run`](../../classes/Engine.md) entry point. Executable help/version CLI dispatch is internal to the reference executable and has no equivalent in the game runtime API. |
| [`enum_value ERR_INVALID_DATA [Error] = 30`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | [Pinned value](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml#L2748). [ADR 0018](../../decisions/core-data-io.md#adr-0018) distinguishes invalid configuration encoding (`DecoderFallbackException`) from malformed text (`FormatException`) at [`ConfigFile.Load`](../../classes/ConfigFile.md#m-electron2d-configfile-load-system-string). The pinned [PackedDataContainer.pack declaration](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/PackedDataContainer.xml) explicitly declares return code 30. Its [owning coverage row](PackedDataContainer.md) is `Blocked` pending the first typed packed-asset container and loader slice under [ADR 0013](../../decisions/resources.md#adr-0013) and [ADR 0023](../../decisions/packed-scenes.md#adr-0023). |
| [`enum_value ERR_INVALID_DECLARATION [Error] = 41`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | [Pinned value](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml#L2781). [ADR 0091](../../decisions/scripting.md#adr-0091) leaves invalid C# declarations to compile diagnostics. The runtime [`Script`](../../classes/Script.md) represents compiled source/type metadata and has no generic declaration-state error. |
| [`enum_value ERR_INVALID_PARAMETER [Error] = 31`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | [Pinned value](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml#L2751). [ADR 0028](../../decisions/rendering.md#adr-0028) exposes invalid ports or connections as `ArgumentException` from [`VisualShader.ConnectNodes`](../../classes/VisualShader.md); a general error integer loses the operation context. |
| [`enum_value ERR_LINK_FAILED [Error] = 38`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | [Pinned value](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml#L2772). [ADR 0091](../../decisions/scripting.md#adr-0091) uses compiled C# factories and assemblies for [`Script`](../../classes/Script.md). Compilation/link failures belong to the build or the shader import boundary under [ADR 0028](../../decisions/rendering.md#adr-0028); no generic runtime link status is exposed. |
| [`enum_value ERR_LOCKED [Error] = 23`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | [Pinned value](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml#L2727). [ADR 0018](../../decisions/core-data-io.md#adr-0018) distinguishes an operating-system lock/sharing failure through the original `IOException` from [`ConfigFile.Save`](../../classes/ConfigFile.md#m-electron2d-configfile-save-system-string). Engine object phase guards belong to their own API. |
| [`enum_value ERR_METHOD_NOT_FOUND [Error] = 37`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | [Pinned value](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml#L2769). [ADR 0002](../../decisions/product.md#adr-0002) binds methods through typed C# delegates and events (e.g. [`ElectronObject.PropertyListChanged`](../../classes/ElectronObject.md)). The string-dispatched missing-method status has no corresponding public call; independent protocol codes belong to [`JSONRPCErrorCode`](../../classes/JSONRPCErrorCode.md). |
| [`enum_value ERR_OUT_OF_MEMORY [Error] = 6`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | [Pinned value](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml#L2676). [ADR 0112](../../decisions/text-patterns.md#adr-0112) preserves `OutOfMemoryException` when [`RegEx.Compile`](../../classes/RegEx.md#compile) cannot allocate native pattern storage. The CLR reports its managed allocation failures separately. |
| [`enum_value ERR_PARAMETER_RANGE_ERROR [Error] = 5`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | [Pinned value](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml#L2673). [ADR 0049](../../decisions/core-data-io.md#adr-0049) uses `ArgumentOutOfRangeException` with the byte position for an invalid [`XMLParser.Seek`](../../classes/XMLParser.md#seek) offset. |
| [`enum_value ERR_PARSE_ERROR [Error] = 43`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | [Pinned value](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml#L2787). [ADR 0048](../../decisions/core-data-io.md#adr-0048) preserves failed [`JSON.Parse`](../../classes/JSON.md) with an error line and message; [ADR 0018](../../decisions/core-data-io.md#adr-0018) throws `FormatException` from [`ConfigFile.Parse`](../../classes/ConfigFile.md#m-electron2d-configfile-parse-system-string) with the failed line. |
| [`enum_value ERR_PRINTER_ON_FIRE [Error] = 48`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | [Pinned value](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml#L2803). [ADR 0004](../../decisions/product.md#adr-0004) has no product API for this documented easter egg. No built-in reference operation returns the value. |
| [`enum_value ERR_QUERY_FAILED [Error] = 21`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | [Pinned value](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml#L2721). [ADR 0013](../../decisions/resources.md#adr-0013) preserves a typed dependency query in [`ResourceLoader.GetDependencies`](../../classes/ResourceLoader.md#getdependencies). Loader failures propagate from the selected loader, and the empty collection represents an absent dependency list. No common query-failure identity is exposed. |
| [`enum_value ERR_SCRIPT_FAILED [Error] = 39`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | [Pinned value](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml#L2775). [ADR 0091](../../decisions/scripting.md#adr-0091) uses compiled C# behavior through [`Script.New`](../../classes/Script.md); invocation failures are ordinary exceptions. Dynamic script reload readiness is tracked on its own coverage rows. |
| [`enum_value ERR_SKIP [Error] = 45`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | [Pinned value](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml#L2793). [ADR 0001](../../decisions/product.md#adr-0001) leaves skip/no-value results to the operation; for example [ADR 0049](../../decisions/core-data-io.md#adr-0049) makes [`XMLParser.SkipSection`](../../classes/XMLParser.md#skipsection) a cursor operation with no returned numeric skip status. |
| [`enum_value ERR_TIMEOUT [Error] = 24`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | [Pinned value](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml#L2730). [ADR 0094](../../decisions/networking.md#adr-0094) publishes deadlines through the request-specific [`HTTPRequestResult.Timeout`](../../classes/HTTPRequestResult.md) result from [`HTTPRequest.RequestCompleted`](../../classes/HTTPRequest.md#requestcompleted). |
| [`enum_value ERR_UNAUTHORIZED [Error] = 4`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | [Pinned value](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml#L2670). [ADR 0018](../../decisions/core-data-io.md#adr-0018) propagates `UnauthorizedAccessException` from [`ConfigFile.Load`](../../classes/ConfigFile.md#m-electron2d-configfile-load-system-string) when caller permissions prohibit reading. Authentication of encrypted configuration has its own `CryptographicException`. |
| [`enum_value ERR_UNAVAILABLE [Error] = 2`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | [Pinned value](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml#L2664). [ADR 0094](../../decisions/networking.md#adr-0094) projects packet absence through [`PacketPeer.GetPacketError`](../../classes/PacketPeer.md#getpacketerror) ([`PacketReadStatus.Unavailable`](../../classes/PacketReadStatus.md)) with an `InvalidOperationException` from [`PacketPeer.GetPacket`](../../classes/PacketPeer.md#getpacket). Unsupported operations keep their domain exceptions. |
| [`enum_value ERR_UNCONFIGURED [Error] = 3`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | [Pinned value](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml#L2667). [ADR 0049](../../decisions/core-data-io.md#adr-0049) gives [`XMLParser.Read`](../../classes/XMLParser.md#read) an `InvalidOperationException` before opening input. An unopened object is distinct from normal EOF. |
| [`enum_value FAILED [Error] = 1`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | [Pinned value](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml#L2661). [ADR 0013](../../decisions/resources.md#adr-0013) preserves the original request failure from [`ResourceLoader.LoadThreadedGet`](../../classes/ResourceLoader.md), alongside `ThreadLoadStatus.Failed` while retained. A general numeric failure would discard the cause. |
| [`enum_value OK [Error] = 0`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | [Pinned value](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml#L2647). [ADR 0018](../../decisions/core-data-io.md#adr-0018) exposes successful [`ConfigFile.Load`](../../classes/ConfigFile.md#m-electron2d-configfile-load-system-string) as normal return; [ADR 0048](../../decisions/core-data-io.md#adr-0048) exposes successful [`JSON.Parse`](../../classes/JSON.md) as true, including a valid JSON null. The global zero status is unnecessary. |
| [`enum_value EULER_ORDER_XYZ [EulerOrder] = 0`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | ADR 0004 excludes the spatial rotation-order consumers Basis.from_euler/get_euler, Quaternion.get_euler and Node3D.rotation_order; Quaternion.from_euler uses fixed YXZ. Ordinary Vector3 data and numeric shader matrices remain applicable under ADRs 0033/0028 and do not consume this enum. No implementation trigger. See [Euler order audit](../euler-order.md). |
| [`enum_value EULER_ORDER_XZY [EulerOrder] = 1`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | ADR 0004 excludes the spatial rotation-order consumers Basis.from_euler/get_euler, Quaternion.get_euler and Node3D.rotation_order; Quaternion.from_euler uses fixed YXZ. | [`enum_value METHOD_FLAG_CONST [MethodFlags] = 4`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | [Declaration](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/core/object/method_info.h#L36-L46). ADRs 0001/0002/0010/0091 retain typed calls/events and explicit cold Script metadata. Bit 4 comes from MethodBind receiver constness and becomes a DocData qualifier; GDScript enum completion also filters it. CLR IsStatic, a getter, PureAttribute and readonly parameters do not establish C++ receiver constness. This language-specific qualifier has no C# counterpart under ADR 0001; query behavior remains on each owning method row. No implementation trigger. |
| [`enum_value METHOD_FLAG_EDITOR [MethodFlags] = 2`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Blocked | [Declaration](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/core/object/method_info.h#L36-L46). ADRs 0001/0002/0010/0091 retain typed calls/events and explicit cold Script metadata. Bit 2 denotes editor-method metadata, not a class ToolAttribute or Engine.IsEditorHint. CLR visibility/attributes do not prove editor eligibility. Trigger: the ADR 0027 editor method discovery/action consumer must define and exercise per-method eligibility shared with Script metadata. No runtime scheduler or invented attribute is added by this audit. |
| [`enum_value METHOD_FLAG_NORMAL [MethodFlags] = 1`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | [Declaration](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/core/object/method_info.h#L36-L46). Typed methods need no binding-registration marker. No implementation trigger. |
| [`enum_value METHOD_FLAG_OBJECT_CORE [MethodFlags] = 64`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | — | Excluded | [Declaration](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/core/object/method_info.h#L36-L46). | [`enum_value METHOD_FLAG_VARARG [MethodFlags] = 16`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | [`public System.Reflection.MethodInfo[] GetScriptMethodList()`](../../classes/Script.md) | Implemented | [Declaration](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/core/object/method_info.h#L36-L46). | [`enum_value METHOD_FLAG_VIRTUAL [MethodFlags] = 8`](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/doc/classes/@GlobalScope.xml) | [`public System.Reflection.MethodInfo[] GetScriptMethodList()`](../../classes/Script.md) | Implemented | [Declaration](https://github.com/godotengine/godot/blob/ed1daf0bf001b61586d9930840f2f1394092c079/core/object/method_info.h#L36-L46).
